R34 DIAGNOSTIC — REFERENCE RULES / SONS OF HORUS / NAMES
CAT revision=33

PLACEHOLDER RULES (805):
Daemonic Aura | The model gains a 5+ Invulnerable Save. | Daemonic Aura > Legiones Astartes | r47-wb-favour-aura-rule
Daemonic Mutation | The model gains +1 Attack. | Daemonic Mutation > Legiones Astartes | r47-wb-favour-mutation-rule
Daemonic Strength | The model gains +1 Strength. | Daemonic Strength > Legiones Astartes | r47-wb-favour-strength-rule
Beyond Judgement | Every model is treated as equipped with Trophies of Judgement and the unit gains Fear. Measure the 8-inch Leadership penalty from any model. Trophies penalties are non-cumulative. | Beyond Judgement — Trophies of Judgement & Fear > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r71-nl-bj-5-rule
Cult Arcana — Quickblood | This unit gains Fleet. | Pavoni > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-0-arcana
Cult Mastery — Quickblood | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, it also gains Crusader. | Pavoni > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-0-mastery
Cult Mastery — Kine Shields | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, models instead gain a 5+ Invulnerable Save against shooting; an existing Invulnerable Save improves by 1 against shooting to a maximum of 4+, and Cover Saves against shooting improve by 1 to a maximum of 3+. These benefits do not apply in close combat. | Raptora > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-1-mastery
Cult Mastery — Precognitive Strike | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, it may also re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 when making Overwatch, Return Fire and Stand & Shoot attacks. | Corvidae > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-2-mastery
Cult Arcana — Discipline of the Mind | This unit gains Stubborn and may re-roll failed Pinning tests. | Athanaeans > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-3-arcana
Cult Mastery — Discipline of the Mind | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, it additionally gains Adamantium Will. | Athanaeans > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-3-mastery
Cult Arcana — Ashen Blow | This unit gains Hammer of Wrath. | Pyrae > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-4-arcana
Cult Mastery — Ashen Blow | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, close-combat attacks and Flame weapons used by models in the unit gain Soul Blaze. | Pyrae > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Long March (non-compulsory Troops) > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-long-march-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-4-mastery
Beyond Judgement | Every model is treated as equipped with Trophies of Judgement and the unit gains Fear. Measure the 8-inch Leadership penalty from any model. Trophies penalties are non-cumulative. | Beyond Judgement — Trophies of Judgement & Fear > Legion Terminator Squad — Horus’ First Company > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-first-company-term-r71-nl-bj-5-rule

Cult Mastery — Kine Shields | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, models instead gain a 5+ Invulnerable Save against shooting; an existing Invulnerable Save improves by 1 against shooting to a maximum of 4+, and Cover Saves against shooting improve by 1 to a maximum of 3+. These benefits do not apply in close combat. | Raptora > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Horus’ First Company > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-first-company-term-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-1-mastery
Cult Mastery — Precognitive Strike | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, it may also re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 when making Overwatch, Return Fire and Stand & Shoot attacks. | Corvidae > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Horus’ First Company > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-first-company-term-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-2-mastery

Cult Mastery — Ashen Blow | If this unit has the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule, close-combat attacks and Flame weapons used by models in the unit gain Soul Blaze. | Pyrae > Prosperine Cult > Legion Terminator Squad — Horus’ First Company > Legiones Astartes | r32-soh-first-company-term-r19-cult-r45-cult-terminator-unit-4-mastery
Stealth Adept — whole squad | Every model in the unit gains Stealth. Models in Terminator Armour, Bikes, Jetbikes, Dreadnoughts and Vehicles are not eligible. An attached Independent Character must also possess Stealth Adept or the unit may not benefit while attached. | Stealth Adept — whole squad > Legion Tactical Squad > Legiones Astartes | r71-nl-gen-0-stealth-rule
Beyond Judgement | Every model is treated as equipped with Trophies of Judgement and the unit gains Fear. Measure the 8-inch Leadership penalty from any model. Enhanced Warriors | This squad has been enhanced by Fabius Bile for +3 points per model. After deployment but before the first turn, roll a D6 for this squad: 1 — Berserk Rage: make an Armour Save for every model; remove each failure, and survivors gain +1 Strength. 2–5 — Stable Mutation: every model gains +1 Strength and +1 Initiative. 6 — Created a Monster: every model gains +1 Strength, +1 Initiative and +1 Attack; in missions using Victory Points, surviving models from the squad count as casualties when calculating Victory Points. | Enhanced Warriors > Fabius Bile > Legion Tactical Squad > Legiones Astartes | live-r2-fabius-enhanced-tactical-unit-rule
Stealth Adept — whole squad | Every model in the unit gains Stealth. Models in Terminator Armour, Bikes, Jetbikes, Dreadnoughts and Vehicles are not eligible. An attached Independent Character must also possess Stealth Adept or the unit may not benefit while attached. | Stealth Adept — whole squad > Legion Assault Squad > Legiones Astartes | r71-nl-gen-1-stealth-rule

Imperial Fists — Legion Rules & Armoury | LEGION SPECIAL RULE
DISCIPLINED FIRE
The Imperial Fists are renowned for disciplined marksmanship and the maintenance of carefully prepared fields of fire.
When an Imperial Fists unit makes a First Fire or Overwatch shooting attack, models in the unit may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 when firing:
• Bolters
• Bolt Pistols
• Combi-Bolters
• Storm Bolters
• Heavy Bolters
• the bolter component of a Combi-weapon
FORTIFICATION MASTERS
The Imperial Fists possess an unrivalled understanding of defensive warfare and the construction and reduction of fortifications.
Imperial Fists models receive +1 to Armour Penetration rolls against Fortifications, Buildings, Bunkers and other immobile structures with an Armour Value. A non-vehicle Imperial Fists unit occupying a friendly Fortification or Bunker gains the Stubborn special rule.
This rule provides no benefit while occupying an enemy Fortification.
If the Imperial Fists are the defenders in a mission which permits the placement of Fortifications or Obstacles, they may place D3 additional 6" sections of razor wire or tank traps.
These must be placed wholly within the Imperial Fists deployment zone.
BLIND TO THE RISK
Imperial Fists possess an uncompromising determination which can drive them to continue fighting even when withdrawal would be the wiser course.
In a mission with variable game length, when the battle would normally end, the opposing player may demand that one additional complete game turn be played. After this additional game turn has been completed, the battle ends automatically.
This rule has no effect if the mission has already ended because a specific objective or action has been completed.
IMPERIAL FISTS ARMOURY
The VII Legion maintains vast arsenals of siege equipment, relic weapons and defensive technologies accumulated over the long years of the Great Crusade.
The following items may be selected in addition to the normal Space Marine Armoury where permitted.
SOLARITE POWER GAUNTLET — 30 POINTS
Any Imperial Fists Character with access to the Space Marine Armoury may purchase a Solarite Power Gauntlet for +30 points. A model already equipped with a Power Fist may exchange it for a Solarite Power Gauntlet for +5 points.
Weapon | Strength | Special Rules
Solarite Power Gauntlet | 10 | Power Weapon, Unwieldy, Specialist Weapon
Unlike a normal Power Fist, the Solarite Power Gauntlet always strikes at Strength 10 regardless of the bearer's Strength characteristic.
VIGIL PATTERN STORM SHIELD
An Imperial Fists Independent Character may purchase a Vigil Pattern Storm Shield for +25 points.
The bearer may carry no more than one weapon in addition to the shield.
A Vigil Pattern Storm Shield grants a 3+ Invulnerable Save.
The shield occupies one hand and the bearer may never receive the bonus Attack for fighting with two close-combat weapons.
Other Imperial Fists units may gain access to Vigil Pattern Storm Shields through their individual army list entries.
The Vigil Pattern Storm Shield is unique to the Imperial Fists and does not make Storm Shields generally available to the Legiones Astartes.
TELEPORTATION TRANSPONDERS
Any Imperial Fists unit composed entirely of models wearing any form of Terminator Armour may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +15 points per unit.
An Imperial Fists Independent Character wearing any form of Terminator Armour may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +10 points.
A model or unit equipped with Teleportation Transponders may deploy using Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit Deep Strike. An Independent Character intending to Deep Strike as part of another unit must purchase Teleportation Transponders separately.
IMPERIAL FISTS RITE OF WAR:
THE STONE GAUNTLET
The Stone Gauntlet is the embodiment of the VII Legion's doctrine of unyielding defence.
Warriors advance shoulder to shoulder behind interlocking shields, absorbing the fury of the enemy before crushing resistance beneath a methodical counter-assault.
EFFECTS
WARDERS OF THE PHALANX
Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
Legion Breacher Siege Squads may also fulfil compulsory Troops selections normally.
SHIELD WALL
An Imperial Fists Infantry unit in which every model is equipped with either a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield may form a Shield Wall. The Shield Wall is active provided that:
• the unit did not Advance during its preceding Movement phase;
• the unit is not Falling Back;
• the unit did not charge during the current player turn.
While the Shield Wall is active, models in the unit may re-roll failed Invulnerable Saves granted by Boarding Shields or Vigil Pattern Storm Shields. A re-rolled save may never be re-rolled again.
UNYIELDING LINE
While its Shield Wall is active, the unit gains the Stubborn special rule and may re-roll failed Pinning tests.
THE HAMMER BEHIND THE SHIELD
Models equipped with Boarding Shields gain the Hammer of Wrath special rule.
This uses the normal ProHammer definition of Hammer of Wrath.
A model does not receive an additional attack; instead, one of its normally allowed attacks may be resolved at Initiative 10 during a turn in which it charges.
LIMITATIONS
The army's compulsory Troops choices must be selected from:
• Legion Breacher Siege Squads
• Phalanx Warder Squads
The Detachment must include at least one Independent Character equipped with either a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield.
No unit in the Detachment may voluntarily deploy using Deep Strike.
The army may include no more than one Fast Attack choice.
IMPERIAL FISTS RITE OF WAR:
HAMMERFALL STRIKE FORCE
Originally devised for forces deployed from the Phalanx, the Hammerfall Strike Force concentrates elite ship-borne formations for sudden teleport assaults into the heart of the enemy position.
EFFECTS
LANDING FORCE
Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
TELEPORT ARRAY
Any Imperial Fists Infantry unit may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +15 points per unit.
An Imperial Fists Independent Character may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +10 points.
This overrides the normal restriction limiting Teleportation Transponders to models wearing Terminator Armour.
A unit equipped with Teleportation Transponders may deploy using Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit Deep Strike.
An Independent Character intending to Deep Strike as part of another unit must purchase Teleportation Transponders separately.
BLINDING LUMINESCENCE
An Imperial Fists unit arriving by Deep Strike using Teleportation Transponders gains the Shrouded special rule from the moment it is placed on the battlefield until the beginning of its next player turn.
After the unit has been placed, every enemy unit with at least one model:
• within 12" of the arriving unit; and
• with line of sight to at least one model in the arriving unit
must immediately take an Initiative test. If the test is failed, that enemy unit suffers the effects of the Blind special rule:
• Weapon Skill 1
• Ballistic Skill 1
until the end of its next player turn.
Each enemy unit only makes one such test per arriving Imperial Fists unit.
LIMITATIONS
The army's Warlord must be equipped with Teleportation Transponders.
Every Vehicle in the Detachment must begin the battle in Reserve.
The Detachment may not include a Fortification.
IMPERIAL FISTS RITE OF WAR:
TEMPLAR ASSAULT
The Templar brethren represent the martial traditions of the VII Legion at their most aggressive.
Where other Imperial Fists formations hold the line or break fortifications through methodical siegecraft, a Templar assault is a direct application of overwhelming force intended to destroy the enemy in close combat.
EFFECTS
TEMPLAR HOST
Templar Brethren Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
CRUSADING ASSAULT
During an Assault phase in which a Templar Brethren Squad charged after disembarking from a vehicle with the Assault Vehicle special rule during the same player turn, the squad gains the Furious Charge special rule. In addition, its normal charge distance is increased from 6" to 7" for that Assault phase.
SWORDS OF THE LEGION
During the first round of a close combat in which a Templar Brethren Squad charged, models in that squad may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 with:
• Rending Weapons
• Power Weapons
• Relic Blades
• other weapons specifically stated to count as swords
ASSAULT TRANSPORTS
A Templar Brethren Squad may select one of the following as a Dedicated Transport at its normal points cost:
• Land Raider Phobos
• Land Raider Proteus
A Templar Brethren Squad of a size which cannot be carried by either vehicle may instead select a Legion Spartan Assault Tank at its normal points cost.
All normal Transport Capacity restrictions apply.
LIMITATIONS
The army's compulsory Troops choices must be Templar Brethren Squads.
The army may include no more than one Fast Attack choice.
The army may not include a Fortification.
The army's Warlord must be equipped with a Power Weapon, Rending Weapon, Relic Blade or another sword-like close-combat weapon which ignores Armour Saves. | VII Legion — Imperial Fists > Legion > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r25-legion-vii-reference
Sons Of Horus — Legion Rules & Armoury | LEGION SPECIAL RULES
CLOSE RANGE BRUTALITY
The Sons of Horus favour overwhelming close-range assaults in which gunfire and blade work become almost indistinguishable.
At the beginning of the Assault phase, a Sons of Horus unit which is otherwise able to charge may fire at the enemy unit it intends to charge.
Models armed with Rapid Fire, Pistol or Assault weapons may fire.
Template weapons may not be used.
These attacks hit only on a 5+, regardless of Ballistic Skill, and never cause Morale or Pinning tests.
Firing in this manner does not prevent the unit from charging, but it must charge the unit it fired upon.
If casualties leave the target outside charge range, or destroy it completely, the Sons of Horus unit may not declare a charge against another enemy unit.
Instead, it moves D6" directly towards the target's current position, or its final position if it was destroyed.
This movement is not a charge and may not bring the unit within 1" of another enemy model.
TIP OF THE SPEAR
The Sons of Horus excel at decapitation strikes and sudden assaults upon the most important point in the enemy line.
Even if the mission does not normally permit Deep Strike, the following may deploy using Deep Strike:
• one Sons of Horus Infantry unit selected from Elites;
• one Sons of Horus Independent Character, either alone or joined by a Command Squad.
These units must begin the battle in Reserve.
PRIDE OF THE WARMASTER
The Sons of Horus possess an arrogance born of countless victories and the favour of the Warmaster himself
At the end of the final scheduled game turn, the opposing player may demand that one additional complete game turn be played.
Roll a D6. On a 4+, one additional complete game turn is played. This roll may only be made once per battle.
This rule does not extend a mission which has already ended because a specific objective or action was completed.
SONS OF HORUS ARMOURY
As the favoured Legion of the Warmaster, the Sons of Horus enjoyed access to enormous reserves of arms and munitions. Alongside these Imperial weapons survived the brutal traditions of Cthonia, where weapons intended for gang warfare and murder remained prized even amongst the Legion's highest officers.
BANESTRIKE AMMUNITION — 5 POINTS PER MODEL
Sons of Horus Seeker Squads, Justaerin Terminator Squads and Independent Characters equipped with a Bolter, Foeblaster Boltgun, Combi-Bolter, Storm Bolter or Combi-Weapon may purchase Banestrike Ammunition for +5 points per eligible model.
Every eligible model in a squad must purchase the upgrade, or none may do so. Models which have replaced their bolt weapon with another weapon neither pay for nor benefit from Banestrike Ammunition.
When firing Banestrike Ammunition, use the following profile where appropriate:
Weapon | Range | Strength | AP | Special Rules
Banestrike Bolter | 18" | 4 | 4 | Rapid Fire, Banestrike
BANESTRIKE
A natural To Wound roll of 6 made with Banestrike Ammunition is resolved at AP3.
When Banestrike Ammunition is used with a Foeblaster Boltgun, Storm Bolter, Combi-Bolter or the bolter component of a Combi-Weapon, retain the weapon's normal number of shots and firing type, but reduce its range to 18", use AP4 and apply the Banestrike special rule.
Banestrike Ammunition may not be combined with Special Issue Ammunition or another ammunition upgrade.
CTHONIAN CULLING BLADE — 10 POINTS
Any Sons of Horus Independent Character or squad Sergeant with access to the Space Marine Armoury may purchase a Cthonian Culling Blade for +10 points.
Weapon | Strength | Special Rules
Cthonian Culling Blade | User | Rending (5+)
RENDING (5+)
A Cthonian Culling Blade follows the normal ProHammer rules for Rending, except that the effect is triggered on a natural To Wound roll of 5 or 6.
Against Vehicles, Rending is still triggered only by a natural Armour Penetration roll of 6.
A Cthonian Culling Blade does not count as a Power Weapon.
CHAINAXE — 4 POINTS
Any Sons of Horus Character eligible to select a Close Combat Weapon may instead select a Chainaxe for +4 points.
Weapon | Strength | Special Rules
Chainaxe | User | Chainaxe
A Chainaxe follows the normal rules presented in the Legiones Astartes Army List.
TELEPORTATION TRANSPONDERS
Any Sons of Horus unit composed entirely of models wearing any form of Terminator Armour may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +15 points per unit. A Sons of Horus Independent Character wearing any form of Terminator Armour may purchase them for +10 points.
A model or unit equipped with Teleportation Transponders gains the Deep Strike special rule and may deploy using Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit it.
An Independent Character intending to Deep Strike as part of another unit must purchase Teleportation Transponders separately.
SONS OF HORUS RITE OF WAR:
THE LONG MARCH
After Isstvan V, the Sons of Horus formed the spearhead of the Warmaster's advance upon Terra, an all-conquering host that drove relentlessly through the Imperium and swept aside world after world in its path.
EFFECTS
RELENTLESS MARCH
At the beginning of each Sons of Horus player turn, determine the position of the majority of the surviving models in each Sons of Horus Infantry unit. The unit gains the appropriate special rule until the beginning of the next Sons of Horus player turn:
• Within the Sons of Horus deployment zone — Relentless.
• Outside either player's deployment zone — Fleet.
• Within the enemy deployment zone — Crusader.
If a unit is equally divided between two areas, the controlling player chooses which applicable rule it gains.
THE WARMASTER'S PORTION
During the first Sons of Horus player turn, all units in the Detachment may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1.
In addition, Legion Terminator Squads may be selected as non-compulsory Troops choices.
They may not fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
LIMITATIONS
This Rite of War may only be selected by a Traitor Sons of Horus Detachment.
The Detachment may not include models with the Slow and Purposeful special rule unless they begin the battle embarked aboard a Transport or enter play using Deep Strike.
The Detachment may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment.
SONS OF HORUS RITE OF WAR:
THE BLACK REAVING
The Black Reaving embodies the Cthonian heritage of the XVI Legion. Rather than engaging the enemy in a single ordered battle line, Reaver companies and supporting formations encircle their prey, isolate individual formations and fall upon them from several directions like the gangs of ancient Cthonia.
EFFECTS
REAVER ONSLAUGHT
Reaver Attack Squads may be selected as Troops choices and may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
At least one of the Detachment's compulsory Troops choices must be a Reaver Attack Squad.
CTHONIAN ENCIRCLEMENT
After both armies have been selected, nominate up to two Sons of Horus Infantry units. These units gain the Outflank special rule.
Any Dedicated Transport purchased for one of these units also gains Outflank and enters play together with its passengers.
An Independent Character which begins the battle attached to one of these nominated units may Outflank with it.
When one of the two nominated units enters play, the Sons of Horus player may choose the side table edge from which that unit enters instead of rolling to determine the edge. The other nominated unit follows the normal ProHammer Outflank rules.
LODGE CEREMONY
Once per battle, at the beginning of a Sons of Horus player turn, the Warlord may declare a Lodge Ceremony.
Until the end of that player turn, all non-Vehicle Sons of Horus units gain the Fleet special rule.
CUT THEM APART
During the first round of a close combat, a Sons of Horus unit which charged an enemy unit that was already engaged with another friendly Sons of Horus unit may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1.
This benefit applies only during the player turn in which the unit charged.
LIMITATIONS
The Detachment must include a Legion Centurion upgraded to a Master of Signals Consul.
At least one compulsory Troops choice must be a Reaver Attack Squad.
The Detachment must include at least as many Fast Attack choices as Heavy Support choices.
The Detachment may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment. | XVI Legion — Sons of Horus > Legion > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r25-legion-xvi-reference
Word Bearers — Legion Rules & Armoury | LEGION SPECIAL RULES
FANATICAL DEVOTION
The Word Bearers are driven by a religious fervour which allows them to endure hardship and terror through sheer certainty of purpose.
Non-Daemon units with the Legiones Astartes (Word Bearers) special rule may re-roll failed Morale and Pinning tests.
THE DARK SHEPHERDS
The Word Bearers do not march to war without spiritual leaders to guide their warriors.
A Word Bearers Detachment must include at least one Legion Chaplain Consul or Legion-specific Dark Apostle as an HQ selection.
A character specifically stated to count as a Dark Apostle also fulfils this requirement.
RITUAL OF CONSECRATION
Traitor Word Bearers regard every battlefield as sacred ground upon which blood may be offered to the powers they serve.
When a non-Daemon Word Bearers unit completely destroys an enemy unit during the Assault Phase, it may forgo its Consolidation move.
If it does so, nominate one friendly Daemonic Covenant unit currently in Reserve. That unit may re-roll its next Reserve roll.
Only one Covenant unit may receive this benefit each turn.
This rule has no effect in a Loyalist Word Bearers army.
DAEMONIC COVENANT
The Word Bearers were the first Legion to willingly bind the entities of the Warp to their cause. Through sacrifice and profane ritual, they learned to tear openings in reality through which these creatures could be summoned to war. A Traitor Word Bearers army may include a Daemons of the Ruinstorm Covenant Detachment, selected from the Daemons of the Ruinstorm Army List.
DAEMONS OF THE RUINSTORM COVENANT DETACHMENT
Selection | Allowance
HQ | 0–1
Troops | 1–3
Elites | 0–1
Fast Attack | 0–1
Heavy Support | 0–1
An HQ choice is not compulsory, but at least one Troops choice must be selected.
The Covenant Detachment may cost no more than 25% of the army's total points limit and has no minimum points requirement.
Units are purchased using their normal profiles, options, Dominions and special rules from the Daemons of the Ruinstorm Army List.
The Covenant Detachment counts as the army's Allied Detachment and does not require additional permission to use. A Word Bearers army may not include another Allied Detachment unless a rule specifically states otherwise. Covenant units may not fulfil compulsory selections in the primary Word Bearers Detachment and may not provide the army's Warlord. Word Bearers and Covenant units are treated as Fellow Warriors. Independent Characters from one Detachment may not join units from the other.
DAEMONIC SUMMONING
All units in a Daemonic Covenant Detachment must begin the battle in Reserve.
They may not deploy normally, Infiltrate or Outflank, but instead enter play using the following rules.
When a Covenant unit becomes available from Reserve, nominate one friendly Summoning Point already on the battlefield.
The following count as Summoning Points:
Personal Icons, Icons of Chaos Undivided, Accursed Crozii, and any other rule specifically stated to act as a Summoning Point.
Place the centre model of the summoned unit within 6" of the Summoning Point and more than 2" from any enemy model. The unit then enters play using the normal Deep Strike rules.
If no eligible Summoning Point or legal position is available, the unit remains in Reserve and must roll for Reserves again during a later turn.
A Summoning Point may be used while its bearer is engaged in close combat and is not consumed when used. The same Summoning Point may summon multiple units during the battle.
Any Deep Strike Mishap is resolved normally.
Units summoned in this manner follow all normal restrictions for units arriving by Deep Strike.
DAEMONOLOGY
Daemonic Covenant is separate from Daemonology (Malefic). Units created through Conjuration psychic powers are not Covenant units and do not use the Daemonic Covenant rules.
THE DARK BRETHREN
FROM BEYOND
A Word Bearers army using The Dark Brethren may form a far deeper pact with the entities of the Warp.
If the army includes a Daemons of the Ruinstorm Covenant Detachment, the following changes apply:
The Covenant may use the normal Daemons of the Ruinstorm Allied Detachment Force Organisation Chart instead of the restricted Covenant chart. The normal 25% points limit is removed. The Detachment instead follows the normal points restrictions for Allied Detachments.
Word Bearers and the Daemons of the Ruinstorm are treated as Sworn Brothers.
Before deployment, each Daemon unit must be designated as either Manifested or Summoned.
Manifested units deploy and enter play normally using the rules from the Daemons of the Ruinstorm Army List.
Summoned units must begin the battle in Reserve and enter play using the Daemonic Summoning rules above.
A Word Bearers army may still include only one Daemons of the Ruinstorm Allied Detachment.
WORD BEARERS ARMOURY
The armouries of the XVII Legion contain an ever-growing collection of occult weapons, blasphemous relics and artefacts touched by the Warp. Many of these objects would once have been destroyed by the Imperial Heralds themselves, but now serve as symbols of the Primordial Truth.
ACCURSED CROZIUS — 40 POINTS
Any Word Bearers Praetor or Centurion may purchase an Accursed Crozius for +40 points. A model equipped with an Accursed Crozius counts as a Dark Apostle for the purposes of The Dark Shepherds.
Weapon | Strength | Special Rules
Accursed Crozius | User | Power Weapon
The bearer receives a 4+ Invulnerable Save and counts as possessing a Personal Icon for the purposes of summoning Daemons.
A model may never possess more than one Accursed Crozius.
TAINTED WEAPON — SAME COST AS A POWER WEAPON
Any Word Bearers Character eligible to select a Power Weapon may instead select a Tainted Weapon for the same points cost.
Weapon | Strength | Special Rules
Tainted Weapon | User | Specialist Weapon, Tainted Strike
TAINTED STRIKE
Any unsaved wound inflicted by a Tainted Weapon becomes a Massive Wound and inflicts D3 Wounds instead of 1.
A Tainted Weapon is not a Power Weapon and does not ignore Armour Saves.
BURNING LORE — 30 POINTS
Any Word Bearers Praetor, Centurion, Chaplain or Diabolist which is not already a Psyker may purchase Burning Lore for +30 points.
The model becomes a Psyker with Mastery Level 1 and selects one psychic power from either the Biomancy or Telepathy discipline.
The model follows all normal ProHammer rules for Psykers.
HEX-BOLTS — 5 POINTS PER UNIT
Any Word Bearers Infantry unit equipped with Bolt weapons may purchase Hex-Bolts for +5 points per unit.
Bolt Pistols, Bolters, Combi-Bolters, Storm Bolters and the Bolter component of Combi-Weapons carried by models in the unit gain the Soul Blaze special rule.
Hex-Bolts may not be combined with Special Issue Ammunition or another ammunition upgrade.
ICON OF CHAOS UNDIVIDED — 30 POINTS
One model in a Legion Command Squad, Legion Veteran Squad or Legion Terminator Squad may carry an Icon of Chaos Undivided for +30 points. The Icon counts as a summoning point for the purposes of the Word Bearers' Daemonic Covenant.
In addition, friendly non-Daemon Word Bearers units with at least one model within 6" of the bearer gain the Fearless special rule.
FAVOUR OF THE PANTHEON
One non-named Word Bearers Independent Character in the army may purchase one of the following Favours of the Pantheon. A model may never possess more than one Favour.
Daemonic Aura — 15 points: The model gains a 5+ Invulnerable Save.
Daemonic Mutation — 15 points: The model gains +1 Attack.
Daemonic Strength — 10 points: The model gains +1 Strength.
Daemonic Wings — 20 points: The model becomes Jump Infantry. This may not be combined with a Jump Pack, Bike, Jetbike or Terminator Armour.
Daemonic Visage — 5 points: An enemy unit which loses a close combat involving the bearer suffers an additional -1 Leadership when taking the resulting Morale test.
The effects of a Favour are already included when determining the model's characteristics or special rules and may not be purchased more than once.
WORD BEARERS CONSUL:
DIABOLIST — +35 POINTS
A Word Bearers Centurion may be upgraded to a Diabolist for +35 points.
The Diabolist gains the Daemon and Preferred Enemy (Loyalists) special rules.
A Diabolist may not select a Bike, Jetbike, any form of Terminator Armour, Power Fist or Thunder Hammer.
The presence of at least one Diabolist allows eligible units in the Detachment to purchase Dark Channelling.
A Diabolist remains a Centurion for the purposes of purchasing an Accursed Crozius. A Diabolist equipped with an Accursed Crozius therefore counts as a Dark Apostle for the purposes of The Dark Shepherds.
DARK CHANNELLING — 25 POINTS PER UNIT
If the Detachment includes at least one Diabolist, a Legion Tactical Squad, Legion Veteran Squad, Legion Breacher Siege Squad, Legion Terminator Squad or Legion Assault Squad may purchase Dark Channelling for +25 points per squad.
After both armies have deployed but before the first turn begins, roll a D6 separately for each unit with Dark Channelling:
1–3 — Zealot: The unit gains the Zealot special rule for the duration of the battle.
4–5 — Unholy Strength: Models in the unit gain +1 Strength for the duration of the battle.
6 — Daemon: The unit gains the Daemon special rule for the duration of the battle. The unit may no longer count as a Scoring Unit. In a mission using Victory Points, the unit also counts as destroyed at the end of the battle even if it survives.
The effects of Dark Channelling apply only to the models which form part of the upgraded squad. Independent Characters or other models which subsequently join the unit do not gain its effects.
WORD BEARERS RITE OF WAR:
THE DARK BRETHREN
Before the corruption of the XVII Legion became manifest, the Word Bearers were renowned for their cohesion and self-sacrifice. After their fall, these same qualities were turned towards darker purposes, entire warbands offering blood and souls to the powers beyond reality in exchange for their favour.
EFFECTS
ARCH-TRAITORS
All Word Bearers Independent Characters in the Detachment gain Preferred Enemy against models belonging to the Loyalist faction.
SIGNS AND PORTENTS
After deployment but before the first turn begins, select one Word Bearers Troops unit and roll a D6.
On a 1–3, all enemy units gain Preferred Enemy against the selected unit for the duration of the battle.
On a 4–6, the selected Word Bearers unit gains Preferred Enemy against all enemy units for the duration of the battle.
FROM BEYOND
A Word Bearers Detachment using this Rite may include an Allied Detachment selected from Codex: Chaos Daemons. This Allied Detachment is treated as Sworn Brothers with the Word Bearers.
This does not prevent the Word Bearers from using their normal Daemonic Covenant rules.
HELL FOLLOWS WITH THEM
Whenever an enemy Psyker suffers a Wound from Perils of the Warp, that Wound becomes a Massive Wound and inflicts D3 Wounds instead of 1.
LIMITATIONS
This Rite may only be selected by a Traitor Word Bearers Detachment.
The Detachment must include at least one Diabolist.
The Detachment may include no more than one Heavy Support choice.
The army may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment drawn from another Space Marine Legion.
Any Allied Detachment other than Chaos Daemons is treated as Desperate Allies.
WORD BEARERS RITE OF WAR:
LAST OF THE SERRATED SUN
The Serrated Sun were the first of Lorgar's sons to willingly walk the Path of the Primordial Truth. Their survivors retained the Chapter's mastery of orbital assault warfare, descending upon their enemies alongside the monstrous Gal Vorbak in sudden and overwhelming attacks.
EFFECTS
COMPANY OF MONSTERS
Gal Vorbak Dark Brethren may be selected as Troops choices and may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
Every Gal Vorbak unit in the Detachment must purchase either a Legion Drop Pod or Dreadclaw Drop Pod as a Dedicated Transport.
DROP ELITE
Any Word Bearers Infantry unit which may normally purchase a Rhino as a Dedicated Transport may instead purchase a Legion Drop Pod at its normal points cost.
BURNING SUN
Whenever a Legion Drop Pod or Dreadclaw Drop Pod belonging to this Detachment arrives using Deep Strike, every enemy unit with at least one model within 12" of the vehicle's final position must immediately take a Pinning test.
A unit need only take one Pinning test for each arriving Drop Pod, regardless of how many of its models are within 12".
LIMITATIONS
This Rite may only be selected by a Traitor Word Bearers Detachment.
All Infantry units in the Detachment must begin the battle in Reserve and enter play using a Deep Striking Drop Pod, Teleportation Transponders or embarked aboard a Transport with the Flyer type.
The army may not include Immobile units.
The army may not include a Fortification or an Allied Detachment. | XVII Legion — Word Bearers > Legion > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r25-legion-xvii-reference
The Stone Gauntlet | The Stone Gauntlet is the embodiment of the VII Legion's doctrine of unyielding defence.
Warriors advance shoulder to shoulder behind interlocking shields, absorbing the fury of the enemy before crushing resistance beneath a methodical counter-assault.
EFFECTS
WARDERS OF THE PHALANX
Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
Legion Breacher Siege Squads may also fulfil compulsory Troops selections normally.
SHIELD WALL
An Imperial Fists Infantry unit in which every model is equipped with either a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield may form a Shield Wall. The Shield Wall is active provided that:
• the unit did not Advance during its preceding Movement phase;
• the unit is not Falling Back;
• the unit did not charge during the current player turn.
While the Shield Wall is active, models in the unit may re-roll failed Invulnerable Saves granted by Boarding Shields or Vigil Pattern Storm Shields. A re-rolled save may never be re-rolled again.
UNYIELDING LINE
While its Shield Wall is active, the unit gains the Stubborn special rule and may re-roll failed Pinning tests.
THE HAMMER BEHIND THE SHIELD
Models equipped with Boarding Shields gain the Hammer of Wrath special rule.
This uses the normal ProHammer definition of Hammer of Wrath.
A model does not receive an additional attack; instead, one of its normally allowed attacks may be resolved at Initiative 10 during a turn in which it charges.
LIMITATIONS
The army's compulsory Troops choices must be selected from:
• Legion Breacher Siege Squads
• Phalanx Warder Squads
The Detachment must include at least one Independent Character equipped with either a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield.
No unit in the Detachment may voluntarily deploy using Deep Strike.
The army may include no more than one Fast Attack choice. | Imperial Fists Rite of War — The Stone Gauntlet > Rite of War (optional) > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r25-rite-vii-0-the-stone-gauntlet-rule
The Stone Gauntlet | Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops and fulfil compulsory Troops; Breacher Siege Squads may also fulfil compulsory Troops. A shield-equipped Infantry unit may form a Shield Wall if it did not Advance in its preceding Movement phase, is not Falling Back and did not charge this player turn. While active, failed Invulnerable Saves from Boarding Shields or Vigil Pattern Storm Shields may be re-rolled; the unit gains Stubborn and may re-roll failed Pinning tests. Boarding Shield models gain Hammer of Wrath using the ProHammer definition. Compulsory Troops must be Breacher Siege Squads or Phalanx Warders. The Detachment must include at least one Independent Character with a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield. No unit may voluntarily deploy by Deep Strike. Maximum one Fast Attack choice. | Imperial Fists Rite of War — The Stone Gauntlet > Rite of War (optional) > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r64-if-r25-rite-vii-0-the-stone-gauntlet-full
Hammerfall Strike Force | Originally devised for forces deployed from the Phalanx, the Hammerfall Strike Force concentrates elite ship-borne formations for sudden teleport assaults into the heart of the enemy position.
EFFECTS
LANDING FORCE
Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
TELEPORT ARRAY
Any Imperial Fists Infantry unit may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +15 points per unit.
An Imperial Fists Independent Character may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +10 points.
This overrides the normal restriction limiting Teleportation Transponders to models wearing Terminator Armour.
A unit equipped with Teleportation Transponders may deploy using Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit Deep Strike.
An Independent Character intending to Deep Strike as part of another unit must purchase Teleportation Transponders separately.
BLINDING LUMINESCENCE
An Imperial Fists unit arriving by Deep Strike using Teleportation Transponders gains the Shrouded special rule from the moment it is placed on the battlefield until the beginning of its next player turn.
After the unit has been placed, every enemy unit with at least one model:
• within 12" of the arriving unit; and
• with line of sight to at least one model in the arriving unit
must immediately take an Initiative test. If the test is failed, that enemy unit suffers the effects of the Blind special rule:
• Weapon Skill 1
• Ballistic Skill 1
until the end of its next player turn.
Each enemy unit only makes one such test per arriving Imperial Fists unit.
LIMITATIONS
The army's Warlord must be equipped with Teleportation Transponders.
Every Vehicle in the Detachment must begin the battle in Reserve.
The Detachment may not include a Fortification. | Imperial Fists Rite of War — Hammerfall Strike Force > Rite of War (optional) > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r25-rite-vii-1-hammerfall-strike-force-rule
Templar Assault | The Templar brethren represent the martial traditions of the VII Legion at their most aggressive.
Where other Imperial Fists formations hold the line or break fortifications through methodical siegecraft, a Templar assault is a direct application of overwhelming force intended to destroy the enemy in close combat.
EFFECTS
TEMPLAR HOST
Templar Brethren Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
CRUSADING ASSAULT
During an Assault phase in which a Templar Brethren Squad charged after disembarking from a vehicle with the Assault Vehicle special rule during the same player turn, the squad gains the Furious Charge special rule. In addition, its normal charge distance is increased from 6" to 7" for that Assault phase.
SWORDS OF THE LEGION
During the first round of a close combat in which a Templar Brethren Squad charged, models in that squad may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 with:
• Rending Weapons
• Power Weapons
• Relic Blades
• other weapons specifically stated to count as swords
ASSAULT TRANSPORTS
A Templar Brethren Squad may select one of the following as a Dedicated Transport at its normal points cost:
• Land Raider Phobos
• Land Raider Proteus
A Templar Brethren Squad of a size which cannot be carried by either vehicle may instead select a Legion Spartan Assault Tank at its normal points cost.
All normal Transport Capacity restrictions apply.
LIMITATIONS
The army's compulsory Troops choices must be Templar Brethren Squads.
The army may include no more than one Fast Attack choice.
The army may not include a Fortification.
The army's Warlord must be equipped with a Power Weapon, Rending Weapon, Relic Blade or another sword-like close-combat weapon which ignores Armour Saves. | Imperial Fists Rite of War — Templar Assault > Rite of War (optional) > Army Configuration > Legiones Astartes | r25-rite-vii-2-templar-assault-rule
The Long March | After Isstvan V, the Sons of Horus formed the spearhead of the Warmaster's advance upon Terra, an all-conquering host that drove relentlessly through the Imperium and swept aside world after world in its path.
EFFECTS
RELENTLESS MARCH

• Within the Sons of Horus deployment zone — Relentless.
• Outside either player's deployment zone — Fleet.
• Within the enemy deployment zone — Crusader.
If a unit is equally divided between two areas, the controlling player chooses which applicable rule it gains.

During the first Sons of Horus player turn, all units in the Detachment may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1.
In addition, Legion Terminator Squads may be selected as non-compulsory Troops choices.
They may not fulfil compulsory Troops selections.

Source Entry | Adherent | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 1 | 4 | 2 | 8 | 3+
Adherent Sergeant | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 1 | 4 | 2 | 9 | 3+
GUIDED BY PROPHECY At the beginning of an Assault phase, the squad may take a Leadership test using Leadership 7 regardless of the Leadership of any model in the unit. If the test is passed, until the end of that Assault phase the squad has Weapon Skill 5 and Feel No Pain (6+). If the test is failed, there is no effect.
Force Organisation:
• Troops
Unit Composition:
• 4 Adherents
• 1 Adherent Sergeant
Unit Type:
• Infantry
• The Adherent Sergeant is a Character
Wargear:
• Power Armour
• Combi-flamer
• Bolt pistol
• Chainsword
• Frag grenades
Special Rules:
• Legiones Astartes (Salamanders)
• Support Squad
• Guided by Prophecy
OPTIONS
• The squad may include up to five additional Adherents for +22 points per model.
• For every five models in the squad, one Adherent may replace his Combi-flamer with:
• Heavy Flamer ................................. +10 points
• The entire squad may take:
• Krak grenades ............................ +2 points per model
• Melta bombs .............................. +5 points per model
ADHERENT SERGEANT
The Adherent Sergeant may replace his Chainsword with:
• Rending Weapon ................................ +5 points
• Power weapon ................................. +10 points
• Power fist .................................... +15 points
• Thunder hammer ............................... +20 points
The Sergeant may take:
• Artificer Armour ............................. +10 points
The Sergeant may select up to 50 points of permitted weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury and Salamanders Armoury.
DEDICATED TRANSPORT

Rite of War Interaction | The Stone Gauntlet is the embodiment of the VII Legion's doctrine of unyielding defence.
Warriors advance shoulder to shoulder behind interlocking shields, absorbing the fury of the enemy before crushing resistance beneath a methodical counter-assault.
EFFECTS
WARDERS OF THE PHALANX
Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
Legion Breacher Siege Squads may also fulfil compulsory Troops selections normally.
SHIELD WALL
An Imperial Fists Infantry unit in which every model is equipped with either a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield may form a Shield Wall. The Shield Wall is active provided that:
• the unit did not Advance during its preceding Movement phase;
• the unit is not Falling Back;
• the unit did not charge during the current player turn.
While the Shield Wall is active, models in the unit may re-roll failed Invulnerable Saves granted by Boarding Shields or Vigil Pattern Storm Shields. A re-rolled save may never be re-rolled again.
UNYIELDING LINE
While its Shield Wall is active, the unit gains the Stubborn special rule and may re-roll failed Pinning tests.
THE HAMMER BEHIND THE SHIELD
Models equipped with Boarding Shields gain the Hammer of Wrath special rule.
This uses the normal ProHammer definition of Hammer of Wrath.
A model does not receive an additional attack; instead, one of its normally allowed attacks may be resolved at Initiative 10 during a turn in which it charges.
LIMITATIONS
The army's compulsory Troops choices must be selected from:
• Legion Breacher Siege Squads
• Phalanx Warder Squads
The Detachment must include at least one Independent Character equipped with either a Boarding Shield or Vigil Pattern Storm Shield.
No unit in the Detachment may voluntarily deploy using Deep Strike.
The army may include no more than one Fast Attack choice. | PHALANX WARDER SQUAD > Legiones Astartes | r42-role-vii-0-effects-warders-of-the-phalanx-phalanx-warder-squads-rite-note
Rite of War Interaction | Originally devised for forces deployed from the Phalanx, the Hammerfall Strike Force concentrates elite ship-borne formations for sudden teleport assaults into the heart of the enemy position.
EFFECTS
LANDING FORCE
Phalanx Warder Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
TELEPORT ARRAY
Any Imperial Fists Infantry unit may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +15 points per unit.
An Imperial Fists Independent Character may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +10 points.
This overrides the normal restriction limiting Teleportation Transponders to models wearing Terminator Armour.
A unit equipped with Teleportation Transponders may deploy using Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit Deep Strike.
An Independent Character intending to Deep Strike as part of another unit must purchase Teleportation Transponders separately.
BLINDING LUMINESCENCE
An Imperial Fists unit arriving by Deep Strike using Teleportation Transponders gains the Shrouded special rule from the moment it is placed on the battlefield until the beginning of its next player turn.
After the unit has been placed, every enemy unit with at least one model:
• within 12" of the arriving unit; and
• with line of sight to at least one model in the arriving unit
must immediately take an Initiative test. If the test is failed, that enemy unit suffers the effects of the Blind special rule:
• Weapon Skill 1
• Ballistic Skill 1
until the end of its next player turn.
Each enemy unit only makes one such test per arriving Imperial Fists unit.
LIMITATIONS
The army's Warlord must be equipped with Teleportation Transponders.
Every Vehicle in the Detachment must begin the battle in Reserve.
The Detachment may not include a Fortification. | PHALANX WARDER SQUAD > Legiones Astartes | r42-role-vii-1-effects-landing-force-phalanx-warder-squads-rite-note
Rite of War Interaction | The Templar brethren represent the martial traditions of the VII Legion at their most aggressive.
Where other Imperial Fists formations hold the line or break fortifications through methodical siegecraft, a Templar assault is a direct application of overwhelming force intended to destroy the enemy in close combat.
EFFECTS
TEMPLAR HOST
Templar Brethren Squads may be selected as Troops choices.
They may fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
CRUSADING ASSAULT
During an Assault phase in which a Templar Brethren Squad charged after disembarking from a vehicle with the Assault Vehicle special rule during the same player turn, the squad gains the Furious Charge special rule. In addition, its normal charge distance is increased from 6" to 7" for that Assault phase.
SWORDS OF THE LEGION
During the first round of a close combat in which a Templar Brethren Squad charged, models in that squad may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 with:
• Rending Weapons
• Power Weapons
• Relic Blades
• other weapons specifically stated to count as swords
ASSAULT TRANSPORTS
A Templar Brethren Squad may select one of the following as a Dedicated Transport at its normal points cost:
• Land Raider Phobos
• Land Raider Proteus
A Templar Brethren Squad of a size which cannot be carried by either vehicle may instead select a Legion Spartan Assault Tank at its normal points cost.
All normal Transport Capacity restrictions apply.
LIMITATIONS
The army's compulsory Troops choices must be Templar Brethren Squads.
The army may include no more than one Fast Attack choice.
The army may not include a Fortification.
The army's Warlord must be equipped with a Power Weapon, Rending Weapon, Relic Blade or another sword-like close-combat weapon which ignores Armour Saves. | TEMPLAR BRETHREN SQUAD > Legiones Astartes | r42-role-vii-2-effects-templar-host-templar-brethren-squads-rite-note

GUIDED BY PROPHECY At the beginning of an Assault phase, the squad may take a Leadership test using Leadership 7 regardless of the Leadership of any model in the unit. If the test is passed, until the end of that Assault phase the squad has Weapon Skill 5 and Feel No Pain (6+). If the test is failed, there is no effect.
